Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The elementary operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 401. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 395 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on elementary structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 401. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 395. QED.
